# Native meshing remediation register

Working register for `docs/plans/native-meshing-remediation.md`. Generated from the 2026-10-10/11 audit, profiles and
test-integrity review; status columns are updated at phase gates (status only, no run logs). It remains through final
qualification and is deleted in Phase Q's final cleanup.

The final plan's decisions override implementation sketches preserved in raw reviewer findings:
- Package-owned immutable records use `StrictModule` (D8), even where a finding says dataclass or `NamedTuple`.
  Ephemeral mutable tool ledgers and internal C++ structs are not scientific records.
- Tools never import `tests/_support`; tools own corpus descriptors, tests own fixtures and assertions.
- Native threads are requested only through `MeshingExecutionPolicy.parallelism`; an outer `ResourceRequest.cpu_cores`
  is an admission ceiling, not a second request.

Status values: `open`, `in-progress`, `done`, `closed: declared-capacity refusal (user decision §5.7)`,
`decided:<choice>`, `rationale` (kept above threshold with a written reason), `superseded` (raw reviewer suggestion replaced by a plan decision), and `rejected` (finding refuted, with reason). A declared-capacity closure records an honest refusal and its diagnostic demand evidence, not positive qualification.

## 6. Oversized functions (AGENTS mandatory thresholds: CC > 40, cognitive > 50, or > 200 lines)

Cognitive values come from complexipy joined by name and can be misattributed between same-named functions; length and CC are authoritative. `new` marks functions absent at the merge base.

Systemic patterns:

- [C8] ×25: Q1 Resource-control vocabulary: ~6 concepts (work units, geometry queries, cavity cells, scratch/host bytes, wall seconds, published entity counts) implemented by 11+ types and 4 mechanisms: native C scope (phx_mc_execution_*), Python ledgers (NativeExecutionB Fix: Consolidate to three names: (a) `*Limits` = immutable declared maxima (StrictModule, static fields, `limits_id`), fields always `maximum_<noun>`; (b) `NativeExecutionScope` (rename of NativeExecutionBudget) = the single live ledger, constructed only via `MeshingLimits.native_scope()` / `MeshCertificateLimits.native_sco
- [C3] ×82: Q2 Evidence vocabulary: 520 *Evidence/*Report/*Certificate/*Record/*Ledger/*Receipt/*Witness/*Proof/*Claim/*Authority/*Theorem classes in scope (pre-branch 438; +82 on branch: +47 Evidence, +7 Record, +6 Certificate, +6 Witness, +4 Proof, +3 Receipt, +3 Ledger Fix: Taxonomy of four roots, each with a naming rule: `*Limits` (declared maxima), `*Evidence` (measured counters/status of one executed phase; must carry status + at least one measured quantity), `*Certificate` (result of a decision procedure; must carry a tri-state status and the identities it binds), `*Record`/`*Spec` (i
- [C5] ×20: Q3 Hidden state: 12 ContextVars in scope, all branch-new (pre-branch 0): _active_execution_budget + _active_host_workspace (_meshcore.py:969,973), _COORDINATE_BUDGET (_coordinate_enclosure.py:596), _context (fem/_restoration.py:32), two 'original_*_root_prepar Fix: Keep exactly two ambient scopes (native execution scope and host workspace, which must be ambient because nested FFI owners cannot be threaded through C callbacks) and make every other item explicit: pass `CoordinateEnclosureBudget` as a parameter from the 3 entry points that own it (it is already an argument-shaped ob
- [C5] ×1044: Q4 Private coupling: 1044 from-import edges carrying 1977 underscore names (514 top-level, 530 function-local). Largest cross-package pairs: geometry -> discretization._coordinate_enclosure 35 names/29 edges; lifecycle -> meshing 40 names/2 edges (_meshing_sou Fix: Misplaced owners (move, no shims): _family_host_array/_entities/_ancestry/_vertex_ids/_failure/_budget from meshing/_quad_generation to a new meshing/_host_arrays.py (or _meshcore where the C layout lives) with public names; _mesh_facets/_candidate_pairs/_facet_entity_ids/_EmbeddingState from geometry/_mesh_certificate
- [C5] ×4: Q5 Facades: discretization/__init__.py 9275 lines (pre 4836): _SYMBOL_MODULES 2394 entries (lines 7-3401), TYPE_CHECKING block 2394 names (3419-5981), __all__ 3271 names (6003-9275), _FACADE_EXPORT_MODULES 12 modules (3403-3416); 877 __all__ names have neither Fix: Two listings are the minimum compatible with ty + lazy loading: (1) the TYPE_CHECKING import block (the only thing ty can read; keep it as the human-edited ground truth), and (2) one runtime map `_EXPORTS: dict[str, str]` (name -> relative owner module; `None`/module exports expressed as the module path itself). Derive
- [C1] ×11000: Q6 Lexicon: density of the slop lexicon (actual|original|owning|authoritative|genuine|receipt|witness|premise|exact|complete|canonical|never|merely|rather than...) per kLOC: meshing 26.3, lifecycle 26.6, _meshcore 22.3, geometry 15.8, interchange 9.2, discreti Fix: Rule set (enforceable subset marked *): R1* error message = `<parameter or field> <observed value or kind>; expected <condition>` in one sentence, naming the parameter in backticks; R2* banned as bare modifiers in messages/docstrings: actual, original, genuine, authoritative, owning, honest, real, true, proper, legitim

Systemic patterns:

- [C2] ×30: Lifecycle-mixing monoliths (validate/prepare/execute/commit/publish in one body) above AGENTS thresholds Fix: Per function: extract validate_*, build_*/stage_*, check_*, commit_*/publish_* helpers in the same TU operating on one context struct; keep the extern C body a thin driver; preserve every spend()/charge call, predicate call order and status value. No forwarding helpers.
- [C3] ×60: Positional counters/evidence/failure arrays indexed by literal (counters[4], counts[3]=work.used, failure[0..4], bytes[5], evidence std::array<uint64_t,6/9>) with slot meaning only in header prose Fix: Follow the existing PHX_MC_TRIANGULATION_3D_* model (14 slot macros): add slot macros for every *_COUNTERS/*_FAILURE/*_VISITS family (delaunay work[9]/memory[6], tet_mesh refine/improve/counts, plc3d failure[5], power_cells failure[4], graph_partition, surface_envelope counts[7]); internally fill a named struct and cop
- [C4] ×30: Duplicated geometry/key helpers across translation units: dot/cross (5 files, 3 vector types), same_point (2), collinear vs predicates.hpp collinear3d, representable (2), det3 (4), lift (2), find_root union-find (3), upward/downward nextafter (6), kOrder/kTetF Fix: One geometry3.hpp (double* dot/cross/difference/squared_distance/point_in_domain/next_up/next_down), one exact_scalar.hpp (det3<T>, lift<T>), one topology_keys.hpp (undirected_edge, kTetFaceOrder, index union-find). Migrate every caller and delete local copies; keep Expansion/Dyadic-typed exact variants separate.
- [C3] ×13: C ABI parameter explosion: 12 exported functions with >15 positional parameters; phx_mc_delaunay_2d grew 4->9 params (tests now pass INT64_MAX, INT64_MAX, INT64_MAX, nullptr, nullptr) Fix: Group count/pointer pairs into size-tagged C structs (phx_mc_source_complex, phx_mc_limits, phx_mc_surface_insert_batch); bump PHX_MC_ABI_CONTRACT; migrate phydrax/_meshcore.py ctypes bindings and tests in the same cutover; no flat-signature shim.
- [C1] ×480: Lexicon-stacked prose (actual/original/authoritative/genuine/owning/premise/witness/receipt) in headers, comments and ABI docs Fix: Rewrite each contract as inputs/outputs/status codes in one sentence per invariant; delete stacked qualifiers and negation-defense clauses. tet_mesh.hpp (171 hits), phydrax_meshcore.h (44), test_refine3d.cpp (74), test_improve3d.cpp (44), improve3d.cpp (34), plc3d.cpp (29).
- [C9] ×60: Magic work-cost literals passed to spend()/charge(): 53 call sites, 11 distinct values (spend(1) x20, spend(4) x14, spend(8) x5, 16, 32, 24, 6, 5, 3, 2, 0) plus work += 7/6/12 and 13*count+42 Fix: Named constexpr cost classes per TU (kWorkPerCavityCell, kWorkPerProofRow, ...) with a one-line cost-model comment; totals unchanged so budget refusal behavior is preserved.
- [C4] ×17: Repeated C ABI entry prologues: MemoryScope construction x6 in tet_mesh.cpp, active-scope check x7 in bounded_memory.cpp, PlcInput assembly x2 in plc3d.cpp, inspect/commit argument validation duplicated in improve3d.cpp Fix: with_native_scope(handle, fn) mirroring improve3d.cpp ready(); with_active_scope(opaque, fn); make_plc_input(...); shared EdgeInsertionRequest validated once for inspect and commit. A table/macro is not needed and would hide the per-entry contract.
- [C3] ×12: Anonymous std::array/tuple records with positional semantics (Stratum = array<int32_t,3>, original_faces_ {f0,f1,f2,source}, parameters[2] meaning depends on stratum kind, BoxOutput 9 positional fields, Input aggregate 17 positional fields) Fix: Named structs with designated initializers; keep byte layout only where exported through the ABI.
- [C5] ×6: Scratch storage and handle destruction bypass the bounded-memory substrate: periodic.cpp, graph_partition.cpp, restricted_delaunay.cpp, arrangement handle use std::vector; periodic_free/arrangement_free use raw delete while 9 other handles use destroy_native_o Fix: [INFERENCE on intent] Either route these through NativeVector/MemoryScope + destroy_native_object like the rest, or state the exemption once in the header contract. Changes refusal behavior under budgets; needs owner decision.
- [C11] ×20: Test-side duplicated fixtures and narration: tet_volume/face_area in test_plc3d duplicate tet_mesh_fixtures.hpp; random_points x3; six_volume/det3 determinant copies; smallest_dihedral vs worst_ratio; 6 std::printf diagnostics inside test bodies; ~45 bare 1e-1 Fix: Move volume/area/determinant/random helpers into tet_mesh_fixtures.hpp (or a test_geometry.hpp beside check.hpp); remove printf narration; name shared tolerances; split the 140-line test_cavity scenario (549-689) by contract.

Systemic patterns:

- [C5] ×14: FFI layer hosts and upper layers mutate hidden preparation/live state through private attributes (budget._parent, budget._imported_preparation_receipts, budget._live_preparations, active._prepared_envelope_sources) plus ContextVar _VOLUME_OPERATION_STARTED; pr Fix: Public meshing/_native_preparation.py NativePreparationLedger attached to the budget by one public method; public parent property only; delete the three dicts and TYPE_CHECKING meshing imports from _meshcore.py; providers import by public name.
- [C4] ×51: Copy-adapted route skeleton: 14 _*_request type-pair guards + 19 isinstance-triple arms in _execute_bound + 19 arms in _plan_bound; budget/receipt/bind block in 5 execute routes; literal enforced_limits/unenforced_limits tuples at 13 publish sites then patched Fix: Keep the single central fail-closed match (AGENTS) but one-line arms: generic typed _route_request[S,T]; one native_route_budget(receipt, limits, started) context helper; one publication_policy(route_kind) builder so publish_native_result stops rewriting caller input.
- [C2] ×12: Monolithic bodies over AGENTS thresholds (>200 lines or CC>40): 11 in providers/_native*.py plus retain_owner in _meshcore Fix: Named phase functions returning frozen phase records; keep call order, evidence key order, StrictModule field order.
- [C3] ×45: Anonymous positional records: 15 N-tuple array returns in _meshcore, 10-tuple _source_complex indexed declared[0..9], *_COUNTERS name tuples indexed with .index(), 4-tuple/12-positional-field records in providers, 4-arg positional record_native_phase callback Fix: Frozen slotted dataclasses (as PlcRecovery3D/TetMeshArrays already do); typed counter records built once at the FFI boundary; NativePhaseRecorder Protocol.
- [C4] ×8: Micro-validators re-declared instead of phydrax/_validation.py: _capacity/_limit/_budget/_unsigned_budget/_node_id in _meshcore; _positive_count, inline nonnegative-int, _identifier in providers Fix: Add bounded_integer(value, name, *, low, high) to _validation.py (positive_integer/nonnegative_integer lack an upper bound); replace _identifier with canonical_identifier.
- [C4] ×7: Field-by-field spec/result reconstruction where dataclasses.replace/eqx.tree_at exists (already used at _native_polyhedral.py:188) Fix: replace(spec, field=...); values unchanged so fingerprints stable, verify prepared_id goldens.
- [C1] ×300: Slop lexicon in docstrings/messages: 148 hits in _meshcore, ~45 periodic, ~50 _native_layer (73/kLOC), ~60 raise sites in _native/sources/publication; messages name no field or expected value Fix: Rewrite as '<field> must <condition>; got <value>'. No pytest match= pins the quoted strings (grep tests/); 'creating thread'/'single-use' wording appears in tests/unit/geometry/test_meshcore.py and tests/unit/test_execution_memory_sampling.py and must be checked.
- [C5] ×90: Private cross-module imports between sibling providers and from geometry/discretization/linalg/optim privates (~90 names), plus function-local imports (17 in _native_surface.py, 6 _native_layer.py, 4 _native_publication.py, 3 _native_sources.py) Fix: Promote shared helpers to public names in their owners; hoist local imports once the ledger owner removes cycles; delete the reverse import _periodic.py -> providers._native_periodic.
- [C4] ×25: Phase timing uses two conventions: manual phase_started/record_elapsed pairs (curve 5, planar 5, surface 6, _implicit 4) vs with measure_phase (dual >=9) Fix: Migrate every manual pair to measure_phase.
- [C3] ×4: Stringly issue accumulators (list[str] prose appended in long if-chains, sometimes fed by try/except converting helper errors to strings) Fix: Frozen SupportIssue(code, detail) records formatted at the raise site; drop the try/except at _native_polyhedral.py:95-140.

Systemic patterns:

- [C2] ×9: Lifecycle-mixing monoliths: validate/prepare/execute/certify/publish in one body Fix: Split each by the AGENTS phase list into helpers that return small NamedTuple/dataclass phase records; keep one fail-closed dispatch; preserve exception order and fingerprint dict keys.
- [C4] ×19: Field-by-field reconstruction of records instead of one owner constructor Fix: _UniformAllowance.from_limits(limits); _HostEpoch.seed(...)/from_forest(...) classmethods; one builder for the 20-kwarg geometry-transition inputs.
- [C5] ×108: Cross-module _private imports and function-local imports as dependency glue Fix: Promote the shared bisection host kernel and surface-state kernel into owner modules with non-underscore names imported at module top; hoist every local import that is not a genuine cycle (the _bisection ones in _device_adaptation are not).
- [C5] ×19: Ambient ContextVar budget read + None-guard repeated per helper Fix: Resolve the ledger once at the executor boundary and carry it on _SurfaceState / pass explicitly.
- [C4] ×8: Ad-hoc nonnegative/positive integer validation inline instead of _validation Fix: Use phydrax._validation.nonnegative_integer / positive_integer per named budget (also yields messages that name the field).
- [C6] ×37: RuntimeError impossible-state guards and 'lost its ... owner' messages Fix: Return non-Optional values from owners so the guard is a type fact; where a guard is real, one _require_ended_evidence(budget) helper.
- [C1] ×175: Lexicon-stacked docstrings/messages (actual/original/authoritative/genuine/complete) Fix: Rewrite to name the offending field and the expected condition; drop adjectives. Coupled tests: tests/unit/meshing/test_surface_metric.py:661,1658; test_bisection.py:603; test_mixed_adaptation.py:897,1006,1370.
- [C3] ×12: Positional/anonymous records: counts[0..3], 9-tuple native results, Any dict records, stringly field lists Fix: Small NamedTuples (_OperationCounts, _ForestArrays, ClipResult) and typed _HostEpoch transforms.

Systemic patterns:

- [C2] ×12: Lifecycle monoliths above AGENTS decomposition thresholds (phase mixing, mutable accumulators, positional result constructors) Fix: Split each by lifecycle phase (validate inputs / prepare carrier / execute loop step / certify / assemble record), each helper returning a named frozen record; keep the loop and the fail-closed dispatch in the parent; preserve exception type+message order and fingerprint payload order.
- [C5] ×180: Function-local imports of _private names to dodge cycles Fix: Move shared leaf helpers (_faces/_triangles/_prepare_identity/_scope, _reserve_fraction_work, _family_host_array, CoordinateEnclosureBudget accessors) into dependency-free owner modules with public names; hoist imports to module top; per-file counts: _layer_core_mapped 33, _implicit_volume 20, _polyhedral 20, _initial_
- [C5] ×9: Hidden lifecycle state via private attributes of another module's object + ContextVar Fix: Give NativeExecutionBudget public methods remember_preparation(receipt)/is_live(prepared)/operation_started and move the registry + clock there; key by evidence_id/prepared_id, not id(); delete the _private helpers from _volume_generation and the _COORDINATE_BUDGET cross-package reads.
- [C3] ×12: Anonymous tuple records and string-keyed accumulators crossing function boundaries Fix: Introduce frozen dataclass/NamedTuple records (RestrictionBatch, CandidateBatch, RefinementTotals, RefinedCarrier, Organization, SupportVerdict) at the producer; consumers read by name.
- [C4] ×18: Micro-helpers re-declared per module instead of shared owner Fix: One owner each: _validation.positive_integer/normalized_identifier for _positive/_text; MeshingScope factory in _scope.py for the 4 _scope copies; one compliance_failure builder in _contracts; one SupportWork.spend; import geometry._meshing_domain._boundary_cell_ribbon_bounds.
- [C1] ×380: Lexicon-laden error messages that do not name the field/condition; pinned by tests Fix: Rewrite as '<field/entity> <observed> but <expected>'; split compound or-checks so each raises its own message; update the 33 match= strings in 18 tests/unit/meshing files in the same change.
- [C6] ×45: Defensive bloat: impossible-state RuntimeError, repeated checkpoints, getattr/hasattr probes, try/except re-wrapping Fix: Remove checks of invariants the constructor/owner already guarantees; one checkpoint per phase boundary; explicit field access; let owners raise MeshingFailure directly.
- [C7] ×11: Type erosion: object/Any params on exported entry points and helpers Fix: Use ConvertibleToArray | None, NDArray[np.float64], Callable[[np.ndarray], np.ndarray]; replace achieved: object with a typed union.
- [C4] ×3: Copy-adapted parallel route orchestration Fix: Extract the shared generate/limits/core-size/publish tail into one function parameterized by the mapped step; make the audit-policy difference an explicit argument; one budget-borrow prologue helper for the 6 'must borrow the actual active budget' sites.

Systemic patterns:

- [C5] ×300: Function-local imports used pervasively to dodge cycles; same symbol re-imported in many functions of one module Fix: Per module, hoist every import whose target does not import back; where a real cycle exists (_periodic<->_topology_edit/_adaptation, _association<->_volume_generation/_plc_planar_support) move the shared symbol down to a leaf module (e.g. meshing/_entity_keys.py, meshing/_host_banks.py) and import at top. One cutover p
- [C5] ×32: Private cross-module helper hub: `_quad_generation` privates and `_volume_generation._entity` act as the de-facto shared substrate; other modules reach into private fields/methods of foreign classes Fix: Create one public-within-package owner (meshing/_host_banks.py) for `family_host_array`, `entities`, `ancestry`, `budget`, `failure`, `entity_key`; delete the underscore names in _quad_generation/_volume_generation; expose `owners` property on ExactPlcCellGeometryConvexSource and a public universe-comparison method on
- [C4] ×7: Collective consensus/barrier logic duplicated: failure-category allgather x3, region-consensus x2, two unrelated `_collective_status` Fix: One `require_collective_success(failure, *, stage, message)` in a leaf module (e.g. meshing/_collective_barrier.py) replacing both helpers and the inline copy (10 call sites). One `_stratum_region_consensus(mesh, dimension, regions, top_dimension)` replacing `_plc_consensus`/`_consensus`. Rename host-side `_collective_
- [C4] ×18: Association routes copy-adapted: lineage guard, per-dimension `next(... if _target_dimension(...) == d)` lookup, UNKNOWN-relation check, GeometryAssociation assembly re-implemented in planar/PLC/surface/implicit/mapped/composed routes Fix: Add two owners in _association.py: `require_transfer_lineage(source, lineage, target, *, degrees)` (single message) and `association_for_dimension(source, dimension)`; migrate all routes; delete the local copies.
- [C2] ×22: Lifecycle monoliths over AGENTS hard thresholds (CC>40 / cog>50 / >200 lines) mixing validate/prepare/execute/certify/publish Fix: Split by lifecycle phase into named helpers that own one invariant each; keep a single orchestrator; preserve exception type/message/order and digest composition order; characterization tests on fingerprints first.
- [C4] ×12: Micro-validators redeclared per module instead of shared `_validation`; `_positive` has three different semantics under one name Fix: Use `_validation` positive_integer/nonnegative_integer/finite positive scalar/identifier/text; delete local copies; rename `_tetra_metric._positive` (an orientation predicate) to `_positively_oriented`.
- [C3] ×11: Anonymous N-tuple returns, positional mega-constructors, magic native counter indices, twin records Fix: Return NamedTuple/StrictModule records; name counter slots as IntEnum beside the ABI declaration; construct evidence records with keywords; merge the 27-field twin restart records behind one placement field.
- [C6] ×55: Defensive/dynamic access: getattr probing, RuntimeError for internal invariants, setattr exception side channel Fix: Replace getattr(field) loops with explicit typed field access or `eqx.tree_at` with named lambdas; carry receipts as a typed constructor field on MeshingFailure/CutCornerLinkError; use ValueError/TypeError per AGENTS error contract for 31 RuntimeError sites (grep tests for pytest.raises(RuntimeError) first).
- [C1] ×180: Lexicon-stacked messages/docstrings that do not name the offending input (actual/original/authoritative/exact/owning/witness) Fix: Rewrite each to `<argument> must be <type/condition>; got <value>`; grep tests for match= before editing (none found for the sampled strings). Note _restart_distribution's 131.8/kLOC is dominated by the legitimate domain noun 'receipt' (131 hits); its real slop is ~45 'actual/exact/authoritative' hits.
- [C4] ×17: Same guard/checkpoint block repeated inside one module Fix: Table-driven guard helper in certify_cell_mesh; one `certify_stage(limits, record_phase, fn)` in _compartments; single type check at DFS entry in require_valid; one `_check_control` in _semantic_controls; one closure-budget helper in extract_hex_dominant.

Systemic patterns:

- [C4] ×95: Exact-rational micro-helpers re-declared per module: binary64->Fraction vector lift, rational dot/cross, Fraction->float directed rounding Fix: One leaf module (e.g. phydrax/geometry/_rational.py, next to _exact_polyhedral_geometry's `type Point`) owning rational_vector, rational_dot, rational_cross, float_below/float_above, exact_float; migrate every caller, delete _vector/_fractions/_point/_rational_point/_fraction_down/_fraction_up/_fraction_endpoint/_exact
- [C5] ×105: Function-local imports used to dodge module cycles Fix: Extract leaf modules (_trim_helpers for shared trim/pcurve helpers, _exact carriers) so _intersection <-> _intersection_curve <-> _boolean_* <-> _query form a DAG; hoist all imports to module top; keep at most one documented lazy import per genuine cycle.
- [C2] ×14: Monolithic functions mixing lifecycle phases with mutable accumulators / nested closures Fix: Split by invariant: validation / preparation / search-or-execute / evidence construction; replace nonlocal closures with a small mutable state dataclass; replace positional N-arg record construction with keyword construction.
- [C6] ×26: getattr/hasattr capability probing and try/except control flow Fix: Explicit isinstance/match on the known carrier union or a typed Protocol; explicit bounds/key checks instead of catching AttributeError/IndexError; budget exhaustion returned as status, not caught.
- [C4] ×6: Budget/work ledger classes copy-adapted per module Fix: One _ApproximationBudget (cells/depth/bytes/peak) parameterized by an error factory for the two approximation modules; one boolean _Work owned by _boolean_arrangement; keep the 'cell budget exhausted' substring.
- [C4] ×12: Inline positive/non-negative integer and identifier validators instead of phydrax._validation, with inconsistent error types Fix: Call _validation.positive_integer / nonnegative_integer / positive_finite_float / normalized_identifier; delete _text and the per-constructor loops. Three sites currently raise ValueError for non-int (AGENTS requires TypeError).
- [C1] ×143: Lexicon-heavy error messages that do not name the offending field or expected condition Fix: Rewrite as '<field> must <condition>' naming the value; drop exact/authoritative/original/native/actual. Per-file raise-lines with lexicon: _intersection 38, _intersection_curve 24, _model 22, _planar_arrangement 10, _boolean_arrangement 10, _stationary_isolation 7.
- [C3] ×12: Anonymous N-tuple returns / positional multi-field record construction / string check codes Fix: Frozen dataclass records with keyword construction; Literal aliases for closure kinds.
- [C5] ×7: Ambient mutable state: ContextVars, module-level caches, import-time registry mutation Fix: Pass preparation mappings explicitly from the entry functions; caller-owned cache object; explicit registry literal. Cache keys and _CONSTRUCTION_FIELDS order are fingerprint/payload-sensitive.
- [C7] ×24: object/Any typed parameters and dict[str, object] payloads Fix: Concrete unions/Protocols; TypedDict per fingerprint payload (keep key names).

- [C5] ×115: Private `_name` helpers imported across module boundaries as a de facto API (`_carrier_payload` x11, `_affine_curve_coefficients` x4, `_square_root_interval` x4 from a membership module, `_period_offset` x3) Fix: One public name per shared helper in its canonical owner: `_carrier_payload` -> public in _model (payload unchanged, fingerprints stable); `_square_root_interval`, `_fraction_down/_up`, `_exact_determinant` -> phydrax/geometry/_interval_enclosure or a leaf brep/_exact.py; `_jadd/_jvector/_EdgeData/_FaceData` -> leaf mo
- [C5] ×96: Function-local imports dodging import cycles (96 names) incl. wrapper functions whose only job is to defer an import Fix: Break _patches <-> discretization._coordinate_enclosure by moving CoordinateEnclosureBudget/_COORDINATE_BUDGET/RationalEnclosureError to a leaf module; break _query <-> _stationary_isolation/_affine_query/_containment by moving shared record types down; hoist every import; delete `_coordinate_budget()`/`_rational_enclo
- [C6] ×60: Impossible-state RuntimeError guards ('X lost its Y') re-checking constructor-owned facts; `model.geometry is None` re-tested in every consumer Fix: One BRepModel accessor (e.g. `exact_geometry`) raising a single ValueError naming model and field; consumers that validated at construction store the narrowed geometry; delete the 29 'lost its' RuntimeErrors (none pinned by pytest.raises match).
- [C2] ×11: Monolithic bodies mixing validate/prepare/search/certify/publish phases with mutable accumulators and nested closures Fix: Split by lifecycle phase into helpers returning typed records; keep iteration order, exception type/message order and fingerprint inputs byte-identical.
- [C3] ×35: Anonymous records: dict[str, Array] hit records, N-tuple returns, positional 6-17 field construction, 17-element parallel tuples in eqx.tree_at Fix: Frozen dataclass / StrictModule record per kind with keyword construction and one stack helper; fixed field order where records flow through jit.
- [C4] ×14: Field-by-field reconstruction instead of an owner replace path Fix: BRepModel replace-subset method in _model.py (3 callers); `_faces_from_patches`; named CurveSurfaceCorrespondence outcome constructors; one `_instance_namespace_fingerprint` (3 copies).
- [C4] ×22: Micro-logic copied verbatim (alias merge x7, offset-chain Fraction frame proof x6, remaining-budget block x3, same-cut predicate x2, exact determinant x2, directed rounding x2) Fix: `_merge_alias`; `_offset_chain` + exact-frame helper; remaining-budget helper; `_cuts_coincide`; single exact determinant owner.
- [C4] ×8: Hand-rolled scalar/identifier validation where phydrax/_validation.py exists Fix: Use canonical_identifier / positive_integer / nonnegative_integer; delete `_integer`, the `_text` import, and the isinstance loop in BRepQueryPolicy; keep policy_id fingerprint inputs unchanged.
- [C5] ×4: Hidden ambient state: ContextVars, module-global cache with Lock, post-construction object.__setattr__ on a StrictModule static field Fix: Pass bank / meridian-jet scope explicitly; move certificate cache onto PreparedBRepQuery; certify_on_host as constructor argument (migrate _differentiable and _boolean_material together).
- [C1] ×160: Lexicon prose in docstrings/messages (~160 hits; 'theorem', 'complete exact', 'never ... repair') Fix: Messages name field + expected condition; one-line invariant docstrings. No slice message is pinned by pytest.raises(match=).

Pure imports like `from ...linal
- [C1] ×312: Lexicon-heavy error messages that do not name the offending field/expected condition Fix: Rewrite as '<field> must <condition>; got <value>'. Coupled tests to update in the same change: tests/unit/discretization/test_cell_geometry_storage.py:231,316; tests/unit/discretization/test_periodic_isometry_group.py:74-124; tests/unit/meshing/test_native_exact_plc_lifecycle.py:361; tests/unit/discretization/test_exa
- [C5] ×45: Cross-module private (_x) imports of de-facto shared helpers Fix: Promote _identification_id, _lifted_loops, _exact_periodic_element (imported from ~15 modules), _require_storage_geometry/_require_logical_geometry_lowering, _COORDINATE_BUDGET/_solve_exact, _CLAIM_ULPS to public names in their owner; import at module top.
- [C3] ×120: Anonymous N-tuple returns / tuple-typed records Fix: NamedTuple/StrictModule records for 3+ field returns consumed by name; keep 2-tuples of arrays. Static 5-tuple execution_capacities must keep field order (treedef).
- [C4] ×6: Inline scalar validators duplicating phydrax/_validation.py (positive_integer L76, nonnegative_integer L86) Fix: Call the shared validators; note _exact_plc_geometry raises ValueError where positive_integer raises TypeError for non-ints (deliberate cutover; no test matches 'must be a positive integer').
- [C6] ×28: RuntimeError impossible-state / Optional-field guards Fix: Type the variant precisely (non-Optional per-conformity layout record; parent_mesh: CellMesh) and validate once at the boundary; delete per-use guards.
- [C4] ×9: Field-by-field StrictModule reconstruction / dual-mode constructors with mode-selecting None params Fix: One constructor per mode (classmethod) and a single replace/tree_at path for derived views; preserve field order.
- [C5] ×2: Lazy facade symbol lists duplicated (dict + TYPE_CHECKING + __all__) Fix: Branch-wide convention (35 facades at HEAD, only execution.py at base). Derive __all__ from the dict (`__all__ = sorted(_SYMBOL_MODULES)`) and keep TYPE_CHECKING as the only hand-written list, or generate the facade from one source via tools/generate_public_api_manifest.py.

Systemic patterns:

- [C5] ×38: Private ContextVar `_COORDINATE_BUDGET` read directly across modules; ad-hoc `if _COORDINATE_BUDGET.get() is None: ledger = CoordinateEnclosureBudget(N, sys.maxsize)` ownership blocks with self-recursion and exception translation, despite a public `coordinate_ Fix: Make `_coordinate_enclosure` export one public `current_coordinate_budget()` accessor (raising a typed error when required) and one `owned_coordinate_budget(work, memory)` context manager; migrate all 38 reader modules; delete the self-recursive wrappers and the 10 `sys.maxsize` memory bounds (name one module constant
- [C5] ×540: Function-local imports with no import cycle: `_cell_geometry_transfer.py` imports `_coordinate_enclosure` at module top (line 63) yet re-imports it inside 60 function bodies, and re-imports `Fraction` locally 10x despite the module-level `from fractions import Fix: Hoist every local import whose target is already a module-level import or is cycle-free (verified: `_coordinate_enclosure`, `fractions`, `sys`, `contextlib`); for the remaining true cycles (`fem/_form_elements` <-> `_hybrid_forms`, `iga/_adaptive` -> `meshing._decision`) move the shared leaf into a lower module and imp
- [C5] ×2: Lazy facades carrying three hand-maintained copies of the export list (`_SYMBOL_MODULES` dict, `if TYPE_CHECKING:` import block, `__all__`) Fix: Keep one ordered mapping as the single source; derive `__all__ = list(_SYMBOL_MODULES)`; keep the TYPE_CHECKING block only if ty needs it (otherwise generate it from the mapping with the existing tools); delete `_FACADE_EXPORT_MODULES`. Restore the copyright header. finite_volume: 1954 -> ~1000 lines; fem: 1247 -> ~650
- [C2] ×14: Monolithic lifecycle functions above AGENTS hard thresholds (CC>40 / cog>50 / >200 lines) mixing validate-prepare-certify-publish, several with 11-27 parameters and one production caller Fix: Phase-split by lifecycle (validate / prepare / certify / publish) with a frozen request/config record replacing the kwarg lists; keep one fail-closed selector dispatch per router; preserve exception type/message/order.
- [C3] ×338: Anonymous positional tuple returns and tuple-typed records (`-> tuple[`), including a 13-element `tuple[Any, ...]` geometry record and five parallel `corner_source_*` arrays Fix: NamedTuple/frozen dataclass per multi-field return at module boundaries; keep `tuple[float, float]` (value, error) pairs only where the pair is the mathematical object. Prioritise the 13-tuple and the >3-field ones.
- [C1] ×519: Lexicon-laden messages/docstrings (actual/original/authoritative/owning/witness/premise) that do not name the offending field or expected condition Fix: Rewrite each message as `<field> <observed> ; expected <condition>`; drop adjectives. Scouts found no `pytest.raises(match=)` coupling for the sampled chart/fem messages; `tests/unit/discretization/test_mixed_adaptation.py:1370` matches 'retained original source atlas' [INFERENCE on owner function].
- [C4] ×25: Micro-validators and exact-geometry micro-helpers re-declared per module instead of `_validation`/one leaf: `_positive` vs `_positive_integer`, `_orientation` x2 byte-identical, `_area`/`_exact_area`, `_wedge_sign` x2, integer-monomial integral x3, `domain = ' Fix: One exact-triangle leaf module (orientation/area/contains/map), one `_reference_domain(kind)` in `_coordinate_enclosure`, positive-int via shared `_validation`, one Fraction monomial integral with domain enum; delete copies in the same change.
- [C6] ×37: Impossible-state `raise RuntimeError` guards and re-validation of constructor-guaranteed invariants (e.g. 'changed type' checks on immutable StrictModule fields) Fix: Type the fields concretely so narrowing is static; keep a single typed guard at each public entry; delete the rest.
- [C7] ×30: Type erosion: `object`-typed StrictModule fields/params and `tuple[Any, ...]`/`dict[str, Any]` records Fix: Concrete owner types under TYPE_CHECKING; NamedTuple records; drop the runtime isinstance re-checks they currently require.

Systemic patterns:

- [C4] ×176: Rebuild-by-type dispatch: 8 `_rebuild_*/rebuild_*` functions re-call each type's constructor with the node's own fields to force re-validation; generic signature-based reconstruction already exists and is used only as a fallthrough Fix: One `reconstruct(node)` owner (the existing signature/fields-driven path at _meshing_sources.py:1500-1525) applied by default; a small explicit table ONLY for types whose constructor needs external bindings (meshes/results/theorems/implicit_geometries) or a derived-field equality check. Branches that are literally `X(n
- [C1] ×430: Lexicon error messages that do not name the offending field/value (actual/original/exact/complete/owning/authoritative/receipt/theorem) Fix: Rewrite each message as `<path>.<field> must <condition>; got <summary>` (the `path` string is already threaded through `visit`). Keep exception types. Only two fragments are test-coupled: 'Registered exact PLC geometry|exact PLC source does not bind' and 'Layer certificate' (tests/unit/meshing/test_meshing_source_auth
- [C5] ×190: Private cross-module imports and function-local imports used to reach other packages' internals and dodge cycles Fix: Lifecycle must not know 40 modules' private constructors: move reconstruction responsibility to owners (see pattern 1), which removes most `_x` imports. Break the _meshing_sources<->_meshing_source_families/_meshing_field_records and _restart_topology<->_distributed_checkpoint cycles by moving shared helpers (`_native_
- [C7] ×90: Untyped boundaries: `Any`/`object` parameters, `Mapping[str, Any]` closures keyed by 19 string roles, N-tuple returns (3/6/8/12-tuples) Fix: Frozen result records (dataclass/StrictModule) for every multi-value return; a typed `MeshingSourceClosure` record replacing the role-keyed dict (keep persisted key names in the codec); typed `_PartitionMeasurements` for the 12-tuple.
- [C2] ×22: Functions over AGENTS hard thresholds (CC>40, cog>50, >200 lines) with no documented rationale Fix: Split by lifecycle phase (collect/validate/rebuild; carrier-table/topology/assembly; coarsen/allocate/refine/measure). Keep one explicit fail-closed dispatch per function.
- [C4] ×8: Copy-adapted entry points and reporting across the three CAD text codecs Fix: `_cad_io` (already the dispatcher) owns `decode_cad_bytes`/`read_cad_file` parameterized by the format's `decode_*_resource`; `_cad` owns one approximation-loss/report builder. Public names `read_step`/`read_iges`/`read_brep_text` keep their signatures.
- [C4] ×7: Hand-maintained parallel symbol/type lists that must stay in sync Fix: One admission table per owning package (each package exposes its artifact types), one export list per facade, one shared CAD capability constant.
- [C6] ×8: Exceptions as control flow and isinstance re-raise guards Fix: Order `except CadInterchangeError: raise` before `except ValueError`; replace constructor-failure probing with an explicit predicate from the owner; remove the silent `except ValueError: return curve` and `getattr(loop, 'tolerance', 1e-10)` fallbacks.
- [C8] ×60: Vocabulary drift for one concept: theorem / evidence / certificate / receipt / proof / authority / premise all denote CollectiveMeshEvidence-like records Fix: One noun per concept: `evidence` for measured outcomes, `record` for persisted metadata, `equal`/`same_source` for comparisons; rename `require_same_native_scientific_theorem`->`require_same_source_evidence`, `_native_authority_equal`->`_content_equal`.
- [C3] ×6: Graph kernel: anonymous counter slots and duplicated collective blocks inside one 491-line traced closure Fix: Named counter indices (IntEnum or a NamedTuple of arrays), one `_collective_status(status, axis)` helper, pure phase functions called from the closure (same jaxpr).

Systemic patterns:

- [C11] ×20: Test bodies embedded as string scripts run via subprocess; parent asserts only exit code Fix: Move each scenario into importable modules under tests/_support/distributed_meshing/ (plain typed functions, pytest-rewritten asserts, pytest.raises); one shared runner `run_on_forced_devices(module, args, device_count, *, extra_env)` that prints stdout/stderr on failure. Delete every sys.exit(0) success path and env b
- [C5] ×430: Tests import private `_module`/`_helper` names from phydrax (often function-local) Fix: Route assertions through public entry points; where an internal algorithm is the subject, one dedicated white-box module with module-top imports. No re-exports.
- [C11] ×11: Tests delegate oracles to private helpers of `tools/meshing_qualification.py` (29,309 lines). Fix: corpus
  descriptors remain in a tools-owned data module; test-only builders and assertions move to `tests/_support` and test
  bodies. Tests may consume descriptors, but tools never import tests.
- [C2] ×25: Giant multi-contract test items (>100 lines) Fix: One contract per item (AGENTS 'Test suite architecture'); shared setup via module fixtures.
- [C4] ×30: Per-file duplicated fixture builders; no tests/_support meshing module Fix: Create tests/_support/meshing_fixtures.py (grids, boxes, tetra pairs, _scope/_specification/_policy/_carrier builders, _checks); migrate all callers, delete local copies.
- [C11] ×150: pytest.raises match= on generic words, alternations or lexicon prose Fix: Assert exception type plus structured field (category/stage/entity id); keep match only for one distinctive stable token.
- [C11] ×100: Unexplained numeric tolerances and pinned-output literals Fix: Name tolerance constants with one-line derivation (eps multiples as test_sizing.py:42 does) or use exact Fraction equality where values are rational.
- [C1] ×300: Lexicon in test names, comments, assertion messages Fix: Rename to observable contract; state the invariant in plain words.
- [C7] ×38: Any-typed test helper signatures Fix: Annotate with concrete types (CellMesh, TensorGridPlan, Float[Array,...]).
- [C6] ×22: Hand-rolled refusal/narrowing guards instead of pytest.raises / direct access Fix: pytest.raises with category assertion; direct attribute access; one `_require_exact_plc_source` helper.

Systemic patterns:

- [C11] ×36: Giant multi-contract scenario tests (>80 lines, several lifecycle phases, in-body branching on the parametrized value) Fix: Split per contract with one module-scoped published-state fixture; move per-row setup into the parametrize table (pytest.param with ids), no if/elif on the row value in the body.
- [C11] ×81: Bare pytest.raises(ValueError/TypeError) with no match or category assertion Fix: Assert a typed category/check code (MeshingFailureCategory, certificate.check) or one short stable token; parametrize sequences of refusals with ids.
- [C11] ×194: match= coupled to error prose / lexicon / alternations Fix: Match a stable identifier (field name, check code) not sentences; remove alternations by one row per expected diagnostic. Must be done together with the package C1 message rewrite.
- [C11] ×9: Tests importing other test modules' private helpers / reaching past functools.cache Fix: Promote the shared builders (_native_family_generation, _mapped_source, _cube_domain/_cube_query, _triangles/_tetrahedra/_curved/_shear/_adapt, _box, sphere atlas source) into tests/_support modules; delete the per-file copies; no re-export aliases.
- [C11] ×503: Private phydrax module imports (function-local) and private-function monkeypatch / private-attribute identity asserts Fix: Route through phx.meshing/phx.lifecycle public entry points; where a seam is genuinely needed, one documented tests/_support helper. Hoist remaining imports to module top.
- [C11] ×241: Un-provenanced tolerance literals (atol/rtol/abs=, 128*eps, 0.05) Fix: Named module constants with one-line derivation (roundoff budget vs discretization bound), reused per file; derive from domain.tolerance where the package exposes it.
- [C11] ×208: Presence-only `is not None` / isinstance-only assertions Fix: Assert the field value the contract requires; keep narrowing only where the producing API is Optional.
- [C11] ×60: Duplicated fixture builders across meshing test files Fix: _scope x15 files, _kuhn_cube x3, _checks byte-identical x3, cube _LOOPS x8, OCP polygon builder x4, HPCFilesystemProfile+POSIXArtifactRepository x23 repo-wide, ArrayArchiveLimits literal x10, MeshingLimits literal x6, polynomial oracle x3: one owner each in tests/_support, delete copies.
- [C11] ×10: Subprocess scenarios with assertion logic embedded in strings / env boilerplate rebuilt per test Fix: One tests/_support subprocess harness (env + runner) and script files or _support modules returning a JSON summary asserted in-process; keep the process boundary only where multi-rank/cold-restart requires it.
- [C11] ×156: Slop lexicon in test names/docstrings/comments Fix: Rename to behavior (what is refused/preserved and on which field); 31% of test names carry actual/original/authoritative/genuine/owning/exact/witness/receipt/premise.

Systemic patterns:

- [C11] ×9: Test modules import private helpers from sibling test_*.py modules (test-as-library) Fix: Move _cylinder/_six_quads, _curved/_space/_content, _curved_surface_chart_fv_fixture, _accepted, _storage, _periodic_template_source into tests/_support (new cell_meshes.py / mapped_cells.py; existing film_meshes.py for periodic templates); import only from tests/_support; delete the cross-test imports and the self-imp
- [C4] ×22: Fixture builders re-declared per module instead of using/extending tests/_support owners Fix: One owner per concept in tests/_support: cad_models.py gains solid_volume(), geometry_of(), named CAD_LIMITS/FIT policies, with_trim_domain(); new cell_meshes.py owns kuhn_cube/unit_square/bisect/storage; film_meshes.py owns cube tables, icosphere, signed_volume.
- [C11] ×60: Giant multi-contract test bodies (>=90 lines, 4-9 contracts, name lists them with _and_) Fix: Split per AGENTS 'one coherent contract per item': share setup via module fixture/_support builder, one test per contract (publication identity vs transfer accuracy vs refusal), parametrize genuine matrices with ids.
- [C5] ×130: White-box coupling: private phydrax modules/symbols imported and monkeypatched in tests Fix: Test private kernels in owner-named unit modules only; elsewhere drive behavior through public inputs; replace monkeypatched constants (_MAXIMUM_ROTATION_ORDER) with explicit policy parameters; drop private-cache assertions (_BERNSTEIN_RESTRICTION_BANK).
- [C5] ×250: Function-local imports of stdlib and phydrax modules inside test bodies Fix: Hoist to module top (keep only provider-gated OCP imports after importorskip, as test_cad_brep_text.py does).
- [C1] ×242: Lexicon in test names / docstrings / match strings Fix: Rename to the observable contract; drop actual/original/authoritative/complete/genuine; keep 'exact' only when exact arithmetic is the property under test; match= on a stable short token naming the field, not prose.
- [C11] ×250: Unprovenanced magic tolerances Fix: One named module constant per scale with a one-line provenance (quadrature order, ulp scale, fit tolerance); exact-source tests use exact equality.
- [C6] ×36: Optional-narrowing via `if x is None: raise AssertionError/ValueError/TypeError(prose)` Fix: Use `assert x is not None` (already the dominant idiom, 200+ sites) and delete the prose messages; one shared accessor in tests/_support where the same guard recurs (geometry_of).

are complete. Cleanup is already authorized after those prerequisites.

Unreleased/Fixed; public additions are in Added, and new representation ownership
is ID-63–67 above. Existing source-publication laws (ID-37/59), build-addressed
runtime/result identities (ID-41), and DF-04 remain in force; no new scientific
identity law is introduced by accepting their authenticated carriers. The MBAR
reference floor is optional static metadata on internal rank/factor helpers, not a
new RankPolicy field, factor layout or public MBAR tolerance. NorSand rounding,
PLIC summation order, compute-precision kernel synthesis, and the cancellation
repairs can change numerical bits without changing their scientific ID payloads.
No byte-identical compiler-cache or numerical-output claim is made for those fixes.

`c0288c6b45f0a01f3a93f767a66247e566a32a0ddaa2d758521332a18cf48dbe`,
ABI `e20f785f1858340ede1320040722e4fbbb0f26edfcabeec2bf26729f82f417c6`,

### Q1 test-contract dispositions

The final report retains per-node causes, baseline classifications, focused commands
and evidence. The following are test/oracle repairs, not relaxed physical targets:

| Owning contract | Retained or strengthened invariant |
| --- | --- |
| Recipe sewing names; inherited-entity compile reuse | Canonical lineage construction preserves malformed-lineage refusals; complete per-slot acceptance masks replace obsolete scalar assumptions, retaining owners and warm-zero compilation |
| Two-material VOF face flux | Original relative comparison plus derived `2 gamma_6` weighted-sum roundoff; independent phase-mass balance and single-face corruption rejection retained |
| Incompressible initial-condition boxes | Independent 16-point per-box Gauss–Legendre quadrature with derived roundoff/remainder, all four modes and removed-mean invariant; replaces underresolved midpoint oracle |
| Liénard–Wiechert cancellation components | Original relative accuracy plus analytic `gamma_2` dot-product roundoff; exact out-of-plane zero and transverse-leak rejection |
| Belief LQG | Derived `gamma_3` scalar residual allowance and one-ULP analytic gain check; unsuccessful/uncertified gain still refuses |
| Gaussian process query grids | Same-grid/dyadic exact replay retained; non-dyadic mapped-state `2 gamma_6` allowance plus bitwise shared Brownian-driver identity |
| Stochastic SAA | Original relative term plus two weighted-dot `gamma_6` allowances; independent `math.fsum` public weighted-gradient residual identity |
| Weighted owner coupling | Native GMRES compatibility with independent weighted minimum norm/gauge; separate DenseLU singular/unsuccessful/invalid-derivative refusal, no solver-policy change |
| Radial embeddings | Existing cutoff error bound propagated through independent Bessel magnitudes, original relative term and C2/transform assertions; wrong-distance rejection added |
| FEM periodic scalar inventory | Fixture uses `shifts @ lattice` for its declared nonsymmetric lattice; conservation assertions unchanged |
| Schur refresh | Independent analytic conjugate roots matched bijectively, stricter eigenvalue accuracy and refreshed reconstruction; identity/version/count/status retained |
| Native volume declarations | Published policy/runtime equality and enforced/unenforced separation replace obsolete static row; all previous enforced names/order and exact-use/one-less refusals retained |
| General mean field | Three broad nodes become eight separately collectable energy/gradient, reference-equivalence, stability, electric-response, nuclear-Hessian, grid-translation, PBE and TDDFT contracts; all assertion bodies and the hydrogen fixture are preserved (`Q1_chemistry_contract_map.json`) |

Additional product regressions retain independent analytic or fault-sensitive
coverage in the final report: exact source-edge/map refusal, bounded polynomial
sampling retention, mixed-precision C4/D4 equivariance, stationary NorSand tangent,
reflected PLIC normals, particlewise FEL energy, sub-ULP affine strain energy/force,
uniform-motion radiation, constant-rate VMC, MBAR unreduced pseudoinverse and weak

Memory evidence changes are canonical at ID-63 and CHANGELOG/Contract and identity

### Final chemistry and complexity corrections

The seven user-authorized isolated 48 GiB attempts closed six files without test
failures. The remaining chemistry file OOM was reproduced in electric response in
a fresh process before nuclear Hessian, so prior-test cache accumulation was not
required. The canonical ERI repair (ID-66) completed electric and nuclear responses
in one process without clearing caches; the owning run passed all eight contracts.
Raw RSS observations and the separately enforced scope cap are not interchangeable
memory claims. `Q1_chemistry_report.json` retains phase-separated compiler/runtime
evidence and the original failure. Final integrated Gaussian consumers passed:
two integral scenarios, eight mean-field contracts, two native-electronic cases,
and one correlation case with one provider skip. The strict weak-integer
coordinate regression passed separately; strong float64/float32/int32 smokes
matched the pre-repair scalar kernel after the loop-carry promotion correction.

The edge oracle now separates authenticated corner admission from once-rounded
length publication within the same preparation scope. Autodiff tape preparation
has its own freshness/reuse lifetime; the four-route Jacobian dispatcher retains
operator admission, failure ordering and evaluation accounting at the existing
consumer boundary. Its CC/cognitive/NLOC decrease from 24/38/162 to 23/35/137.
This remaining ordinary-helper exception preserves the inherited error contracts
and explicit route ownership; no severe threshold is exceeded. New helpers meet
the ordinary limits. `Q1_final_complexity.json` supersedes the two recommendations
in `Q1_complexity.json`; unrelated verified metrics remain reusable.

`Q1_final_smoke.json` exercises final global-state refresh (roots 3 then 2),
exact-source edge publication and foreign-map refusal, and independently derived
normalized s-orbital full/long/short-range integrals at two range parameters.

The final Q1 source snapshot passed repository typing (`Q_ledger_final_typing.json`)
and all nine static/generated gates (`Q_final_static/results.json`): Ruff,
selectors, checked signatures, import boundaries, facades, public API,
capabilities, theorem generation and prose. Later performance edits must verify
their affected surfaces; these receipts are not blanket acceptance of future code.

Post-Q1 host-cost corrections preserve reservation counters, peaks, refusal
records and source-array identity. Differential runs cover 500, 2,000 and 8,000
iterations; 174 affected consumer cases passed, including dedicated two-device
checkpoint placement. The source/corpus/neurofluid corrections passed 17 focused
cases and their public CLI smokes. Raw failures and subsequent repairs remain
separate in `Q2_qualification_inventory.json`, `Q_cost_verification.json`,
`Q_contract_tests/` and `Q_qualification_repairs_smoke/`.

The authenticated archived-layer source completes its documented lifecycle
without replacing the resource-limited unit-box request. Both neurofluid
benchmarks have successful execution records; filtered tabulator compilation
has zero observed value/gradient difference from uncompiled evaluation, and its
JVP matches the declared gradient action. No original-deadline pass is inferred
from a later diagnostic.

P5 coverage remains tied to its frozen Python source and historical `eee…`
native bytes, not relabeled as current `ccf…` coverage. The 43-case field-restart
measurement passed against that frozen source/native pair. Admitted cross-campaign
transfers retain source hashes and an explicitly bracketed native-stability
inference; they do not claim continuous per-campaign native authentication.
`P5_qualified_coverage/summary.json` reports no unexplained missing sets.

OOM receipts without failing nodes remain infrastructure outcomes until their final
isolated escalation receipts are reconciled; they are not product passes. Skipped-only
optional-provider receipts remain optional-provider skips, not successful execution
or unexplained no-tests results. Declared-capacity and deadline refusals preserve
scientific budgets/tolerances and stay limitations even where user-approved.
This section closes no unrelated Phase Q obligation. Final docs build and durable
content migration/deletion remain the separate plan §15.8 gate.
